## 四、我的错词本（本次实测抓取）

| # | 单词 | 中文 / 辨析 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | assign | 分配、布置（任务）；指派　辨析：assess = 评估 |
| 2 | resent | 怨恨，憎恶　resent doing / resent being done；resend 才是"重发" |
| 3 | thorough | 彻底的，缜密的　对比：through = 穿过 |
| 4 | indifferent | 漠不关心的　be indifferent to |
| 5 | compromise | 妥协，折中　reach a compromise；composition = 作文/构成 |
| 6 | appreciate | 感激；欣赏；领会　I would appreciate it if… |
| 7 | variety | 多样性，种类　vary → variety |
| 8 | application | 申请；应用　apply → application |
| 9 | description | 描述　describe → description |
| 10 | embarrassing / embarrassed | 两 r 两 s；-ing 修饰事（令人尴尬），-ed 修饰人（感到尴尬） |
| 11 | independent | 独立的　in- + depend + -ent |
| 12 | worth | 值得　be worth doing（主动形式表被动） |
| 13 | meet | 遇见；满足　meet the needs；meet with = 遭遇 |
| 14 | accommodation | 住宿，膳宿　两 c 两 m |
| 15 | recommend | 推荐　两 c 一个 m |
| 16 | necessary | 必要的　一个 c 两个 s |
| 17 | disappointed / disappointing | 人用 -ed（感到失望），事用 -ing（令人失望） |
| 18 | argument | 争论；论点　argue 去掉 e + -ment |
| 19 | decision | 决定（名词）　decide → decision |
| 20 | convenient | 方便的　It is convenient for sb to do sth |
| 21 | last | 最后的；最不可能的　the last person to do sth |
| 22 | company | 公司；陪伴　keep sb company = 陪某人 |
| 23 | knowledge | 知识　k 不发音 |
| 24 | effect | 名词：影响，效果　have an effect on |
| 25 | affect | 动词：影响 |

> 带复习打勾表的完整版见 `错词本.md`；Anki 卡见 `错词本-anki.csv`。
